Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection aims to identify sarcastic intent from multimodal content, where inconsistencies between literal meaning and contextual cues often signal irony. This task has attracted increasing research attention. However, accurate detection remains challenging due to instance-dependent modality contributions and misleading semantic consistency, where surface-level alignment masks underlying contradictory intent. Existing methods often rely on fixed fusion strategies and treat sarcasm as generic cross-modal mismatch, limiting their ability to capture subtle sarcasm cues and instance-specific modality interactions. To address these challenges, we propose a novel MSD framework that integrates Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization (SaCR). Specifically, a bidirectional gated interaction module performs cross-modal feature filtering and adaptively calibrates textual and visual contributions at the instance level. A dynamic fusion gate further balances modality importance to generate more robust multimodal representations. Furthermore, SaCR is introduced as a label-aware contrastive regularization objective that encourages semantic consistency for non-sarcastic samples while suppressing misleading consistency in sarcastic cases. The proposed framework is trained end-to-end with a multi-objective learning strategy that jointly optimizes multimodal classification and auxiliary unimodal supervision. Extensive experiments on MMSD and MMSD2.0 demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文提出完整的三部分流水线：(1) 构建包含3,413个真实世界、经PII清洗的查询-代码对开源基准；(2) 设计可复现的评估框架；(3) 训练专用双编码器模型。针对标注数据稀缺问题，利用google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it从公共代码仓库生成784,057个合成三元组进行微调，结合Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL)和隐私感知分词器。评估采用平衡子集宏观、查询加权微观及论坛专用三种指标。

Result: 模型在平衡宏观nDCG@10上达到0.5992，微观指标0.5044，论坛指标0.4617，优于基线架构的0.4932宏观分数和google/embeddinggemma-300m的0.5404。经保守的exact/13-gram重叠审计排除训练-基准重叠样本后，平衡宏观分数为0.6011（微观0.5010），证明重叠并非主导结果的因素。MRL截断至256维保留99.9%检索质量，同时将稠密索引存储和精确相似度计算量减少至三分之一。

Conclusion: 本文验证了在资源稀缺的领域特定语言环境中，通过合成数据微调与MRL技术可以构建有效的代码检索模型。基准测试与模型的公开为1C生态系统的代码检索研究奠定了基础，且MRL维度截断策略在存储效率与检索质量之间取得了极佳平衡。

Abstract: Natural language code retrieval is a rapidly evolving task in computer science. However, the 1C:Enterprise ecosystem combines Russian syntax with highly domain-specific terminology, for which open datasets and specialized models have been virtually non-existent. We present a comprehensive pipeline for 1C code retrieval: an open benchmark of 3,413 real-world, PII-scrubbed query-code pairs, a reproducible evaluation harness, and a specialized bi-encoder. To overcome scarce labeled data, we fine-tune on 784,057 synthetic triplets generated by google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it from public code repositories, using Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and a privacy-aware tokenizer. Because the benchmark subsets differ in size, we report balanced-subset macro, query-weighted micro, and forum-only results. Our model reaches 0.5992 balanced macro nDCG@10, 0.5044 micro, and 0.4617 on forum, versus 0.4932 macro for the baseline architecture and 0.5404 for google/embeddinggemma-300m. Removing every benchmark example flagged by the conservative exact/13-gram overlap audit leaves 0.6011 balanced macro (0.5010 micro), indicating that detected train-benchmark overlap does not explain the headline result. MRL truncation to 256 dimensions preserves 99.9% of retrieval quality while reducing dense-index storage and exact similarity arithmetic by a factor of three.

</details>

*Zhifa Geng,Subin Huang,Hao Guo,Junjie Chen,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式代理校正框架（IPCF），用于不完整多模态情感分析（MSA）。通过门控残差修正逐步精炼从非语言模态构造的语言代理，并依据语言可靠性评分进行自适应融合，同时引入阶段式潜在校正目标来稳定优化轨迹，在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS数据集上超越现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中多模态输入常不完整或被破坏，削弱跨模态互补性并引入误导信息。现有基于代理的方法依赖一次性（one-shot）代理构造来补偿退化的语言信息，但生成的代理在初始化时可能粗糙或不可靠，过早注入此类代理会传播初始误差并损害情感预测。

Method: 1) 从非语言模态构建面向语言的代理表示；2) 通过门控残差校正机制在多模态上下文中迭代精炼代理；3) 根据估计的语言可靠性分数，将校正后的代理与观测的语言表征自适应融合，平衡代理补偿与可信语言证据；4) 引入阶段式潜在校正目标，利用完整语言表征作为训练时的语义锚点，稳定代理精炼轨迹。

Result: 在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS三个基准数据集上，在多种缺失模态设置下，所提出的框架一致优于竞争性基线方法，实现了在不完整输入下的鲁棒情感预测。

Conclusion: 迭代式代理校正相比一次性代理构造能更有效地缓解不完整多模态输入带来的信息退化问题，为鲁棒不完整MSA提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis aims to infer affective states by integrating language, visual, and acoustic cues. However, real-world multimodal inputs are often incomplete or corrupted, which can weaken cross-modal complementarity and introduce misleading information into downstream fusion. Existing proxy-based methods for incomplete MSA commonly rely on one-shot proxy construction to compensate for degraded language information, but the generated proxy may be coarse or unreliable at initialization. Prematurely injecting such a proxy into multimodal reasoning can propagate initial errors and compromise sentiment prediction. To address this limitation, we propose an iterative proxy correction framework for robust incomplete MSA. Our method constructs a language-oriented proxy from non-language modalities and progressively refines it under multimodal context through gated residual correction. The corrected proxy is then adaptively fused with the observed language representation according to an estimated language reliability score, allowing the model to balance proxy-based compensation and trustworthy linguistic evidence. In addition, we introduce a stage-wise latent correction objective that uses the complete language representation as a training-time semantic anchor to stabilize the proxy refinement trajectory. Extensive experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and SIMS under diverse missing-modality settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves robust sentiment prediction under incomplete inputs.

</details>

Jin,Xing Wu,Jinghui Lu,Abdul Samad,Akbar Faruqi,Cesar Caraballo,Cibele Brandão,Dhruva,Gupta,Eunji Jeon,Gabriel Madera-Santiago,Geon Lee,Hugo Toshio Itikawa,Insook Cho,Isabelli Martins,Isarar Siddique,Israr Ahmed,Jihyo Kwak,Kanyakorn Veerakanjana,Luis Guilherme Cardoso,Minjin Kim,Piyalitt Ittichaiwong,Renee Dua,Santiago Gudiño-Rosales,Xiujie Chen,Zeo Lapalus,Zixin Xu,Michihiro Yasunaga,Rex Ying,Heuiseok Lim,Jaewoo Kang,Chanjun Park,Hang Jiang,Ethan Goh,Hyunjae Kim,Edison Marrese-Taylor,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qingyu Chen,Irene Li*

TL;DR: HealMed是一个由23名医学专家历时两年构建的多语言医学评估基准，包含9种语言各1000个示例，涵盖MCQA、NLI和开放式问答三种任务格式。研究发现低资源语言性能下降最显著，最强专有模型跨语言稳定性最佳，而医学专用开源模型未必具备多语言稳健性，且专家翻译修订会实质影响评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型评测大多集中在英语或少数高资源语言，缺乏权威的多语言医学基准。翻译质量如何影响跨语言评估结果不清晰，语言资源与模型性能差距之间的关系也未被系统性研究，需要一个由领域专家验证的高质量标准来可靠评估多语言医学能力。

Method: 从9个数据集精选1000个样本×9种语言，涵盖MCQA、NLI和开放式QA三种任务格式。由9个国家和地区的23名医生和医学专家参与开发，每个翻译由两名精通英语和目标语言的专家进行评估和修订，构建了HealMed基准。

Result: 低资源语言上模型性能下降最明显但幅度因语言和模型而异；最强的专有模型跨语言表现最稳定，而许多开源和医学专用模型显示出更大且不一致的差距；医学专用化本身不保证多语言稳健性；专家修订可能提高或降低性能分数，表明翻译质量对跨语言评测结果有实质性影响。

Conclusion: HealMed揭示了多语言医学评估中语言资源差距的显著性和差异性，指出医学专用不等于多语言稳健，翻译质量本身是影响跨语言评测可靠性的关键因素。该基准为多语言医学大语言模型的公平、可信评估提供了专家审核的高质量标准资源。

Abstract: We present HealMed, an expert-reviewed benchmark for multilingual evaluation of large language models in medicine. HealMed contains 1,000 examples in each of nine languages, drawn from nine datasets and covering three task formats: MCQA, NLI and open-ended QA. The benchmark was developed over two years by 23 physicians and medical experts based across nine countries and regions. Each translation was evaluated and revised by two experts fluent in English and the corresponding target language. On HealMed, performance declined most in low-resource languages, although the size of the gap varied markedly across languages and models. The strongest proprietary models were the most stable across languages, whereas many open-source and medically specialized models showed larger and less consistent gaps. Medical specialization alone did not ensure multilingual robustness. Furthermore, expert revision could either raise or lower measured performance, indicating that translation quality materially affects cross-language evaluation results.

</details>


### [40] [Auditing Cross-Lingual Fairness in Language Model Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20047)
*Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多语言水印评估框架，包含四个核心组件（经验校准的检测阈值、阈值无关的伴随测量、三种质量测量范式、广义熵分解），并在6种水印方案、3个开源生成器、11种语言（覆盖4种文字和8个语系）上验证，发现跨语言差异主要是语系间而非语言特有的，揭示出单语言单范式评估无法发现的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型水印方案的评估几乎完全基于英文文本，且依赖各方案的检测阈值和窄范围质量指标。多语言部署暴露了这些评估设计选择在英文上无关紧要、但在跨语言场景下决定结论的问题，因此需要一套针对多语言场景的系统性评估框架。

Method: 提出四组件评估框架：(1) 按部署场景经验校准的检测阈值；(2) 阈值无关的伴随测量，用于区分校准失败与检测失败；(3) 三种互不重叠的质量测量范式——分布性测量、配对语义测量和参考困惑度测量；(4) 基于广义熵分解的跨语言差异分析，按类型学语系划分。

Result: 框架应用于6种水印方案、3个开源权重生成器、11种语言（跨4种文字系统、8个类型学语系）、以及基础模型和指令微调两种模式。发现单语言单范式评估无法揭示的失败模式，观测到的检测和质量的差异主要出现在类型学语系之间的划分上。

Conclusion: 跨语言公平性差距在水印问题上是语言属性结构性的体现，而非特定语言的偶发现象，表明水印设计需要从类型学层面考虑多语言适应性。

Abstract: Watermarking schemes for large language model output are evaluated almost exclusively on English text using each scheme's detection threshold and a narrow set of quality measurements. Multilingual deployment exposes evaluation-design choices that are inconsequential on English but determine conclusions cross-lingually. We propose an evaluation framework with four components: detection thresholds calibrated empirically per deployment context, a threshold-independent companion measurement that distinguishes calibration failures from detection failures, three disjoint quality measurement paradigms (distributional, paired-semantic, and reference-perplexity), and a generalized-entropy decomposition of cross-language disparity over a typological family partition. Applied to six watermarking schemes, three open-weight generators, eleven languages spanning four scripts and eight typological families, and both base and instruction-tuned regimes, the framework reveals failure modes that single-language single-paradigm evaluation cannot surface. Across detection and quality, observed disparity is predominantly between-family on the typological partition, indicating that cross-lingual fairness gaps in watermarking are structural to language properties rather than idiosyncratic to particular languages.

</details>


### [41] [SABET-QA: Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20083)
*Brahim Touayouch,Mirette Moawad,Dmitry Akulov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了SABET-QA框架，通过迭代多跳推理、双向实体-时间评分和槽感知上下文对齐机制，解决时序知识图谱问答中基于嵌入方法无法处理多步查询的问题，在多个基准数据集上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于嵌入的时序知识图谱问答方法采用单次推理流水线，难以处理需要多步推理的复杂时序查询，缺乏递归推理与中间状态精炼的能力。

Method: SABET-QA包含三个核心组件：1) 双向实体-时间评分机制用于跨多跳迭代精炼推理状态；2) 槽感知上下文化模块将问题语义与时序知识图谱嵌入对齐；3) 可微工作记忆支持渐进式假设精炼，并在可用时利用辅助时序边界进行粗粒度监督。

Result: 在CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ和TimeQuestions四个基准数据集上，相比强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升，尤其在复杂多步时序查询上表现突出。

Conclusion: SABET-QA通过引入迭代多跳推理与双向时序评分，有效解决了传统嵌入方法在TKGQA中单次推理的局限，为复杂时序推理任务提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Question Answering over Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGQA) requires reasoning over time-sensitive facts, yet existing embedding-based methods struggle with multi-step queries due to single-pass reasoning pipelines. We propose SABET-QA, a framework that iteratively refines reasoning states across multiple hops via a bidirectional entity-temporal scoring mechanism and a slot-aware contextualization module that aligns question semantics with temporal KG embeddings. A differentiable working memory enables progressive hypothesis refinement, while auxiliary temporal boundaries serve as coarse supervision when available. Experiments on CronQuestions, Complex-CronQuestions, MultiTQ, and TimeQuestions demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly on complex multi-step temporal queries.

</details>


### [42] [OenoBench: A Wine-Domain Benchmark for Knowledge-Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20106)
*Nikita Khudov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了OenoBench，一个包含3,266道葡萄酒领域多选题的基准测试，跨越六个主题和四个难度等级，搭配严谨的LLM驱动构建管线（事实溯源、策略生成、九智能体审计），并在16个前沿配置上评估，发现准确率53%-84%，揭示推理模式增益的不一致、模型自偏好偏差以及参数化回忆天花板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对葡萄酒领域知识的高质量、可溯源、覆盖面广的评测基准，且现有基准构建过程中LLM常被作为事实来源，导致幻觉与不可信问题。作者旨在构建一个来源权威、审计严密、可复现的领域基准，以系统评估前沿模型的能力，并揭示模型在推理模式、自偏好及回忆能力上的深层特征。

Method: （1）从35个经过来源验证的爬虫从政府注册机构（INAO、TTB、OIV）、同行评审期刊和Wikipedia/Wikidata提取38,104条原子化事实，每条事实锚定URL；（2）采用LLM驱动管线：LLM仅用于重格式化事实和审计结果，不作为事实来源（即"溯源保证"）；（3）问题通过五种策略、五类生成器家族生成，共3,266道选择题；（4）每个问题由九智能体审计，并通过与人工黄金标准集的Cohen's κ校准；（5）在16个前沿模型配置上评估，涵盖专有与开放权重模型。

Result: （i）整体准确率范围53%-84%，o3以83.6%领先；（ii）推理模式增益集中在DeepSeek R1（+6.8pp），Claude Opus和Gemini Pro无增益；（iii）Anthropic在自身生成的题上出现+9pp自偏好，Google出现-8pp反向偏好；（iv）前沿开放权重模型与专有推理模型共享成本-准确率帕累托前沿；（v）所有配置在闭卷可解题目上均提升约33pp，揭示了仅依靠上下文切片才能规避的参数化回忆上限。

Conclusion: OenoBench证明了一个将LLM严格限定为格式化和审计角色（而非事实来源）的可信基准构建范式。评测结果表明：推理模式带来的提升因模型而异并非普适；专有模型存在显著的自偏好偏差；开放权重模型已具备与专有推理模型相当的性价比；模型在参数化回忆上存在共同的瓶颈，此瓶颈需通过检索增强等上下文机制突破。作者以CC-BY-SA-4.0协议开源全部资源以推动复现与社区共建。

Abstract: We introduce OenoBench, a wine-domain knowledge benchmark of 3,266 multiple-choice questions across six pillars (regions, grape varieties, viticulture, winemaking, producers, business) and four difficulty tiers. The corpus is built from 38,104 atomic, source-anchored facts extracted by 35 provenance-verified scrapers from government registries (INAO, TTB, OIV), peer-reviewed journals, and Wikipedia/Wikidata. Our methodological contribution is an LLM-driven pipeline in which language models reformat verified facts and audit the result, but never serve as the source of truth: every claim traces to a URL, every question is generated by one of five strategies across five generator families, and every question is scored by a nine-agent audit calibrated against a human gold sheet via Cohen's $κ$. Evaluating sixteen frontier configurations, we find: (i) overall accuracy spans 53%-84%, led by o3 at 83.6%; (ii) reasoning-mode lift concentrates in DeepSeek R1 (+6.8pp) and is absent in Claude Opus and Gemini Pro; (iii) Anthropic shows +9pp self preference on its own questions while Google shows -8pp inverse preference; (iv) frontier open-weight models share the cost-vs-accuracy Pareto frontier with proprietary reasoning models; and (v) every config gains around 33pp on closed-book solvable items, revealing a parametric-recall ceiling that only the contextual slice avoids. We release corpus, audit findings, human-review app, and construction code under CC-BY-SA-4.0.

</details>


### [43] [When Text and Numbers Disagree: Evidence Arbitration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20116)
*Mattia Carletti,Edward Phillips,Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Patitapaban Palo,Lei Clifton,Danielle Belgrave,Xiao Gu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过一个受控的合成基准研究大语言模型在冲突证据（文本摘要、数值时间序列、外部工具输出）之间进行仲裁的行为，发现LLM的仲裁策略是系统性的而非随机的：对文本与数字有显著偏好、更依赖时间新近性而非可靠性提示、并可能过度依赖外部预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于整合来自不同来源且可能相互冲突的证据（如文本摘要、数值观测、外部工具输出），但目前缺乏对LLM如何在这些异构证据之间进行仲裁的系统性理解。理解这种仲裁行为对于构建可靠的工具增强决策系统至关重要，因为当前LLM可能依赖启发式策略而非理性推理来整合冲突信息。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控的合成基准：用潜在的"风险轨迹"同时生成数值时间序列和自然语言摘要，从而构造出恰好只有一个证据源与真实标签一致的冲突场景。该设计允许独立操纵四个变量：证据模态（文本vs数值）、时间新近性、来源可靠性以及证据来源（出处）。在多个开放权重指令微调模型上评估其仲裁行为。

Result: 研究发现LLM的仲裁行为具有系统性模式：模型表现出明显的文本vs数值偏好差异；对时间新近性的遵循程度高于对显式可靠性提示的遵循；并可能过度依赖外部预测，即使这些预测与直接的上下文证据相冲突。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在整合异构证据时常依赖启发式仲裁策略而非稳健的推理，这暴露了工具增强型决策系统的一个关键失败模式，提示需要更好的机制来确保LLM在冲突证据下做出可靠判断。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in settings where textual summaries, numerical observations, and external tool outputs may provide conflicting evidence. We study how LLMs arbitrate between such sources when they support opposing decisions. To do so, we introduce a controlled synthetic benchmark in which latent risk trajectories generate both numerical time series and natural language summaries, allowing us to construct conflicts where exactly one evidence source is aligned with the ground-truth label. This design lets us independently manipulate modality, temporal recency, source reliability, and evidence provenance. Across open-weight instruction-tuned models, we find that arbitration behaviour is systematic rather than random: models exhibit distinct text-versus-number preferences, follow temporal recency more consistently than explicit reliability cues, and can over-rely on external forecasts even when they conflict with direct contextual evidence. These results suggest that current LLMs often rely on heuristic arbitration strategies when integrating heterogeneous evidence, highlighting a failure mode for tool-augmented decision systems.

</details>

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for automated theoretical computer science (TCS) research, yet existing benchmarks remain far from realistic research settings. We introduce \ourbenchmark, an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLMs on frontier, end-to-end TCS research. \ourbenchmark contains $175$ instances drawn from papers accepted to STOC, FOCS, SODA, and COLT in 2025-2026, preserving paper-specific definitions, assumptions, and proof dependencies, with expert-verified Lean formalizations and proofs. Evaluations of leading LLMs reveal that current models remain far from reliably completing the full research pipeline. In particular, autoformalization is the sharpest bottleneck: the best model achieves only $11.5$ on translating natural-language claims into formal theorem statements, compared with $28.6$ Pass@8 when proving human-provided formal statements. Building on \ourbenchmark, we further develop an automated TCS research framework that generates, formalizes, filters, and proves new claims. Of $64$ generated claims, only $6$ ultimately pass expert evaluation and proof verification, indicating that beyond formalization, limited research taste remains another major barrier to autonomous TCS research.

</details>


### [45] [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169)
*Atsuyuki Miyai,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Task-CoEvolve方法，通过自适应验证任务选择来优化LLM智能体harness（提示/代码框架）。它利用方差加权采样聚焦于agent能力边界附近的任务，并从部分评估中估计全量集性能，在保持最终性能的同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的harness优化方法在每轮迭代中对固定验证集进行全量评估，即使某些任务在harness演化过程中已变得不再具有区分度，仍会产生高昂的评估成本。需要一种方法能够自适应选择有信息量的验证任务，减少不必要的评估开销。

Method: Task-CoEvolve包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于候选harness之间分歧度的观察，利用过去结果的方差加权采样，将评估集中在agent能力边界附近的任务上，且采样分布随harness演化自适应调整；(2) 基于采样概率对部分评估结果进行加权估计，从而在不同迭代间即使评估子集不同也能进行一致的性能比较。

Result: 在在线文本分类和Terminal-Bench 2.1上，Task-CoEvolve一致优于固定子集基线，匹配全量集搜索的最终性能，同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少了80%。

Conclusion: 通过将验证任务与harness共同演化（co-evolve），可以在不牺牲最终优化性能的前提下大幅降低评估成本，为LLM agent harness优化提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to efficient LLM agent harness optimization through adaptive validation task selection. Harness optimization iteratively rewrites the harness code based on validation performance, enabling substantial performance gains without updating the underlying model weights. Existing approaches, however, evaluate a fixed validation set in full at every iteration, incurring substantial evaluation costs even on tasks that become less discriminative as the harness evolves. We propose $\textbf{Task-CoEvolve}$, which co-evolves the validation tasks with the harness by addressing two challenges: selecting informative tasks and estimating full-set performance from partial evaluations. Task-CoEvolve builds on the observation that tasks on which candidate harnesses disagree are more informative for distinguishing among them than tasks that are consistently solved or failed. It uses variance-weighted sampling based on past outcomes to focus evaluation on tasks near the agent's capability frontier, with the sampling distribution adapting as the harness evolves. It then estimates full-set scores from the sampled tasks by accounting for their sampling probabilities, enabling consistent comparisons across iterations despite evaluating different subsets. Experiments on online text classification and Terminal-Bench 2.1 show that Task-CoEvolve consistently outperforms fixed-subset baselines and matches the final performance of full-set search while reducing the number of evaluations during optimization by 80%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Task-CoEvolve.

</details>


### [46] [Inject, Align, Recover: Staged Post-Training for Retrieval-Free Document Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20281)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IAR（注入-对齐-恢复）三阶段后训练框架，用于将固定文档语料库转化为可用的参数化知识，实现无需检索的（retrieval-free）文档问答。IAR通过结构化知识注入、QA行为对齐和通用能力恢复三个阶段，在多个数据集和模型家族上显著优于Vanilla SFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理时无法检索源文档的情况下，往往难以回答关于有界文档集合的问题。需要一种方法将固定语料库转化为可用的参数化知识，使模型在不依赖检索的情况下完成文档问答。

Method: IAR包含三个阶段：（1）Inject——将源文档转换为续写、改写和指令条件重建三种训练目标，实现结构化文档知识注入（区别于常规继续预训练）；（2）Align——使用仅含答案的QA监督信号对注入后的模型进行行为对齐；（3）Recover——将领域适配模型与基础指令模型合并，恢复通用能力。

Result: 在Common Corpus（CC）和CCI数据集上，横跨Llama、Phi、Qwen和SmolLM模型家族，IAR在7/8的数据集-模型组合中四个指标全部优于Vanilla SFT，领域QA准确率平均提升3.6个百分点，通用性能（IFEval、MMLU、MSBench）平均提升12.1个百分点。扩展CC基线显示LoRA和FAPM在个别通用指标上获胜，但在同时达到领先或接近领先的领域内化效果的方法中，IAR保持了最强的通用能力。

Conclusion: IAR通过将知识注入、行为对齐和能力恢复解耦为独立阶段，有效提升了检索无关的文档知识内化性能，在领域QA和通用能力之间取得了良好的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models often fail to answer questions about a bounded document collection when the source documents are not retrieved at inference time. We study this setting as document knowledge internalization: converting a fixed corpus into usable parametric knowledge for retrieval-free question answering. We propose IAR (Inject, Align, and Recover), a three-stage post-training framework that separates structured document knowledge injection, QA behavior alignment, and general ability recovery. Unlike conventional continued pretraining, Inject converts source documents into continuation, rewrite, and instruction-conditioned reconstruction objectives. Align then adapts the injected model with answer-only QA supervision, while Recover merges the domain-adapted model with the base instruction model to recover general capabilities. Across Common Corpus (CC) and CCI, and across Llama, Phi, Qwen, and SmolLM model families, IAR improves the domain-primary domain-general frontier for retrieval-free document internalization. In the main comparison, IAR improves over Vanilla SFT on all four reported metrics in 7 of 8 dataset-model settings, with average gains of 3.6 percentage points in domain QA accuracy and 12.1 percentage points in mean general performance across IFEval, MMLU, and MSBench. Extended CC baselines show that LoRA and FAPM can win individual general metrics, but among methods that also reach leading or near-leading domain internalization, IAR retains one of the strongest general profiles.

</details>


### [47] [Inducing Task Models from Computer-Use Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20319)
*Yucheng Jiang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Ruishi Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务模型归纳（TMI）方法，用于从自然主义的计算机使用痕迹（屏幕截图、鼠标键盘操作）中自动发现潜在任务并诱导结构化任务模型，在受控实验中达到0.974的分组一致性和74.9%的执行步骤重建率，并将外部任务准确率提升30.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算机使用代理进入真实工作场景，需要从被动记录的底层事件中学习实际任务执行方式，组织也需要对这些知识进行审计和复用。然而现实工作是多线程的、目标交错进行的，现有方法要么假设给定任务或单一工作流，要么只产生步骤级摘要而非结构化任务模型，无法满足这一需求。

Method: TMI方法包含两大核心步骤：（i）在无约束的轨迹中发现潜在任务，将并发活动解耦分离；（ii）对每个潜在任务诱导任务模型，该模型将分层目标模型（递归目标分解）与程序模型（执行过程的控制流）配对结合。

Result: 在受控的人类和代理轨迹上，TMI对交错任务的分组与真实标注达到0.974的一致度，并重建了74.9%的观察执行步骤，显著优于最强的工作流归纳基线。在外部任务上，基于TMI任务模型派生的技能将保留任务的准确率提升了30.0%。

Conclusion: TMI能够从自然主义的计算机使用轨迹中有效地发现并结构化建模多线程交错的任务，产生可审计、可复用的任务模型，并对下游技能学习有显著提升，为真实工作场景下的计算机使用代理提供了重要的建模基础。

Abstract: Naturalistic computer-use traces, passively recorded screenshots and mouse or keyboard actions, are a valuable resource for deriving symbolic, auditable, and reusable models of how everyday work is done. Such models matter as computer-use agents enter real work, where agents need to learn how tasks are actually performed, and organizations need to audit and reuse that knowledge. However, inducing such task models is challenging, as activity is observed only as low-level events and real-world work is multi-threaded with interleaved goals. Existing methods assume a given task or a single workflow, and produce step-level summaries rather than structured task models. We introduce Task Model Induction (TMI), which (i) discovers the latent tasks in an unconstrained trace, disentangling concurrent activity, and (ii) for each latent task, induces a task model pairing a hierarchical objective model of recursive goal decomposition with a procedure model of the control flow that organized the execution. Intrinsically, on controlled human and agent trajectories, TMI recovers interleaved tasks with 0.974 agreement against ground-truth groupings and reconstructs 74.9% of the observed execution steps, far more than the strongest workflow induction baseline. Extrinsically, skills derived from TMI's task models improve held-out task accuracy by 30.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [48] [G-CARL: Grounded Checklist-Aligned Reward Learning for Patient-Oriented Medical Report Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20331)
*Shiao Xie,Siyu Chen,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Yujin Wang,Xiandong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出患者导向的医学报告解读（PMRI）任务，并设计了G-CARL框架，一种基于检索验证和清单对齐的强化学习方法，同时构建了MMedReport基准和临床医生三维度评估协议，在事实性准确性和患者需求贴合度上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者对医学报告的个性化解读需求日益增长，这要求模型同时具备基于证据的医学事实性和基于语境的患者沟通能力，但现有的医学视觉语言任务未能充分捕捉这两个双重需求。此外，这两个目标在可验证性上本质不同但又紧密耦合，传统的监督微调和整体强化学习范式难以联合优化。

Method: 提出G-CARL框架：(1) 结合多源检索进行原子化声明验证，确保医学事实性；(2) 采用上下文感知、实例特定的加权检查清单，监督回答对用户需求和表达质量的覆盖；(3) 为事实性、用户需求满足度和表达质量提供结构化监督，同时不限制回答多样性。此外构建了MMedReport真实世界基准和临床医生设计的三维评估协议。

Result: 大量实验表明G-CARL在整体质量、声明级别精确度和检查清单召回率上一致优于现有后训练基线；临床医生的成对偏好评估进一步确认G-CARL生成的解读更准确且更贴合患者需求。

Conclusion: G-CARL通过将医学事实性验证与个性化清单监督解耦后联合优化的方式，有效推动了医学报告个性化解读这一新任务的发展，为医学多模态生成模型提供了新的训练范式和评估基准。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of medical reports has emerged as an increasingly important need among patients. Addressing this need requires both evidence-grounded medical factuality and context-dependent patient communication, yet existing medical vision-language tasks do not adequately capture these dual requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce Patient-oriented Medical Report Interpretation (PMRI), a novel open-ended multimodal generation task that requires models to explain medical reports in accurate and accessible language based on a user's query and dialogue history. These two objectives differ fundamentally in their verifiability, yet remain tightly coupled, making them difficult to optimize jointly under conventional supervised fine-tuning and holistic reinforcement learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose G-CARL, a grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning framework that combines multi-source retrieval for atomic claim verification with context-aware, instance-specific weighted checklists for response coverage, providing structured supervision for factuality, user-demand satisfaction, and expression quality without constraining response diversity. We further construct MMedReport, a real-world PMRI benchmark, along with a clinician-designed three-dimensional evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G-CARL consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in overall quality, claim-level precision, and checklist recall. Pairwise preference evaluation by clinicians further confirms that G-CARL produces interpretations that are more accurate and better aligned with patient needs.

</details>


### [49] [ConceptGuard: Benchmarking Context-Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20338)
*Sahil Kale,Ian Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出概念级"双重用途"（dual-use）概念及其基准ConceptGuard，以评估大模型在有害与良性情境下的知识遗忘能力。现有方法在该基准上表现不佳，暴露出强健遗忘与效用间的严重权衡、上下文敏感度提升有限以及概念级控制一致性差等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM知识遗忘方法与基准基于互不相交的事实对（forget vs. retain）和简单的直接事实回忆来评测，无法反映真实场景中的关键需求——在消除有害行为的同时保留良性且有用的知识。有效遗忘应在概念层面运作，确保完全移除不安全的用途，同时维系其正确有益的用法。

Method: 引入"双重用途概念"定义（同一概念可同时用于有害与良性上下文）；构建ConceptGuard基准，其中forget集与retain集在概念使用上明确互补；评估设计为意图敏感的（intent-sensitive），目标为最大化上下文分离以促进更安全的模型行为；采用ROUGE及概念级指标进行多维度评测。

Result: 现有遗忘技术在ConceptGuard上表现不佳：上下文分离弱、ROUGE和概念级指标差；揭示了强遗忘-效用权衡、上下文敏感性提升有限、以及方法间概念级控制一致性差等问题。

Conclusion: 概念级的遗忘评测更能反映真实世界安全需求，当前方法无法满足这一要求，本工作为设计更贴合实际安全需求的遗忘方法提供了新思路，数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require selective removal of harmful or sensitive knowledge, called unlearning, yet existing methods and benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability completely. Current approaches rely on disjoint forget and retain sets composed of independent facts, and measure success using simple and direct factual recall. This framing fails to capture a key requirement of unlearning, namely the ability to eliminate harmful behaviors while preserving benign and beneficial knowledge. We argue that effective unlearning must operate at the level of concepts, ensuring complete removal of unsafe applications while maintaining their correct and useful usage, thereby achieving conceptually meaningful and complete unlearning. To better evaluate unlearning techniques from such a practical viewpoint, we introduce the notion of dual-use concepts: concepts that can be used in both harmful and benign contexts. Building on these concepts, we construct a benchmark called ConceptGuard where forget and retain sets are explicitly complementary in concept usage. Our benchmark uniquely enables unlearning to be explored and gauged at the level of concepts, instead of sparse facts, and evaluation is intent-sensitive with the goal of maximizing contextual separation to promote safer behavior. We demonstrate that current unlearning techniques perform poorly under this setting, showing weak contextual separation alongside poor performance in ROUGE and concept-level metrics. Our results reveal strong forgetting-utility trade-offs, limited gains in contextual sensitivity, and poor consistency in concept-level control across methods, and provide ideas for unlearning approaches that better align with real-world safety requirements. Our dataset is publicly available.

</details>

### [50] [Beyond linear subspaces: Nonlinear moment matching meets quadratic manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19486)
*Reetish Padhi,Serkan Gugercin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统无关、无需优化的二次流形模型降阶框架，可直接构造二次投影矩阵，并证明生成的低阶模型能匹配全阶系统的非线性矩并保留精确的中心流形映射，确保在特定输入下对稳态输出的渐近跟踪。在传输主导的基准问题上（一维阻尼波方程和对流方程）验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性子空间对于具有慢Kolmogorov n-width衰减的线性控制系统存在固有局限性，而二次流形模型降阶虽有潜力克服这一限制，但文献中缺乏用于构造此类二次逼近的系统理论框架。

Abstract: High-multiplicity flexible job shops involve many copies of a small set of job types that must be scheduled on alternative machines. Fluid relaxations provide scalable workload lower bounds, but their fractional machine allocations do not define feasible schedules for individual jobs. Guided by this repeated structure, we develop type-based cyclic template replay (TCTR), which searches job-type templates using an optimization model whose size does not grow with the number of job copies and replays the selected template on the full instance. Conclusion: 悬挂点在表面积最小化中非本质作用，可以将寻找面积最小的Meissner多面体约化为无悬挂点生成集的情形，简化了后续优化搜索的维度。

Abstract: Meissner polyhedra are constant-width bodies obtained from extremal finite sets of unit diameter. This article studies whether these points can play an essential role in surface-area minimization. Given an extremal set we show that the smallest surface area among the Meissner polyhedra based on it cannot increase by deleting a dangling point. </details>

Abstract: This paper proposes a controller design method for shaping the controllability Gramian into a desired form to design the effect from exogenous inputs to the system state. In addition, by deriving a semidefinite programming formulation via a linear matrix inequality (LMI), computational efficiency is improved and additional LMI constraints can be incorporated. When the exogenous input is modeled as Gaussian white noise, the proposed framework is closely related to $H_2$ control, which can be interpreted as a special case of optimal transport. Numerical examples demonstrate anisotropic controllability design for a guidance robot and verify the ability to impose additional directional constraints through LMIs. The numerical examples also confirm that the proposed method approaches $H_2$ control as the desired Gramian tends to zero.

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian methods are effective for nonlinear equality-constrained optimization, but solving their nonlinear primal subproblems can be expensive. For smooth nonconvex problems with deterministic or stochastic objectives, we propose a nonlinear-residual linearized augmented Lagrangian method (NR-LALM) that replaces this subproblem by a regularized Gauss-Newton-type step while retaining the classical multiplier update based on the nonlinear constraint residual. The resulting step is computed from one symmetric positive-definite linear system, but the mismatch between the linearized primal model and the nonlinear-residual update produces a quadratic constraint-linearization error in the multiplier identity. We show that this error can be controlled under local regularity; multiplier boundedness and trajectory localization are derived rather than assumed. With fixed, accuracy-independent parameters, deterministic NR-LALM finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) pair in $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iterations and first-order oracle evaluations. For stochastic objectives, a projected stochastic path-integrated differential estimator with safeguarded restarts requires, in expectation, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ stochastic-gradient evaluations and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ constraint and Jacobian evaluations. Compactness and a Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition further yield finite-length convergence of the deterministic primal-dual sequence. An optional minimum-norm second-order correction reduces the constraint-linearization error from second to fourth order without changing the complexity orders. All theoretical results are formalized in Lean 4. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted error orders and show favorable performance on high-dimensional deterministic and stochastic problems.

</details>


### [57] [To Control or not to Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19862)

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带控制休假（control vacations）的M/M/1排队控制模型，替代传统永久控制方案。在控制期（指数分布时长）结束后启动休假，休假期间使用固定服务率μ。研究目标是找到充分条件，使平均期望成本低于仅使用固定服务率的模型，并构造相应的成本降低策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统排队控制模型通常假设控制是永久性的，但在实际系统中，持续进行服务率控制可能带来更高的控制和调度成本。本文引入"控制休假"机制，即在控制期后允许系统回到固定服务率运行一段时间，从而在成本与控制效果之间取得平衡，提升经济性。

Method: 基于Kanavetas等人的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架；推导充分条件保证平均期望成本可降低；利用相关过程的性质构造成本降低策略；将特定策略下的受控休假过程建模为更新报酬过程，并使用Tauberian定理将平均期望成本与消失折扣法（vanishing discount method）关联，解析求解平均期望成本降低的下界；最后通过仿真验证实际成本降低效果。

Result: 导出了成本可降低的充分条件，构造了可降低平均期望成本的策略，并解析确定了平均期望成本降低的理论下界；仿真结果显示这些策略在实际中能够达到积极的成本降低效果。

Conclusion: 带有控制休假的排队控制模型可以在满足特定条件下显著降低平均期望成本，为排队控制系统的经济性设计提供了新的理论依据和实用策略。

Abstract: We introduce a model with control vacations instead of standard queueing control systems with permanent control. The researched model is an M/M/1 queue with temporary periods of service rate control with two available service rates. After a control period of exponentially distributed length, a control vacation is initiated during which a fixed service rate $μ$ is used. The start of the next control period needs to be scheduled directly at a certain cost. We will use the Markov Decision Process from Kanavetas et al. arXiv:2605.31573 to find a sufficient condition that ensures that the average expected cost can be reduced w.r.t. the model that only uses the fixed service rate. Under this condition we will use properties of this related process to construct a cost reducing policy. The process with control vacations under specific policies induces a renewal reward process. We use a Tauberian theorem to relate the average expected cost of this renewal reward process to a vanishing discount method and analytically determine a lower bound of the average expected cost reduction. Finally, we study the actual attained average cost reduction for these policies through simulation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微分平坦性的高效前馈转向输入计算方法，用于带n个拖车的车辆系统。通过组合四种技术（角度中间变量、子映射因子化、重缩放高阶乘积/商规则、基于截断形式幂级数的复合规则），算法复杂度降至O(r³)，在r=40阶导数下计算时间仍小于1毫秒，远优于符号微分（SymPy在r=14超时）和Bell多项式方法（r=24超时），并以20拖车泊车场景作为端到端验证。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带n个拖车的车辆前馈转向输入通常通过微分平坦性计算，但标准方法（对平坦输出进行迭代符号微分）的表达式规模随n超指数增长，即使借助计算机代数系统在n≈5时就变得不可行，亟需可扩展的高效算法。

Method: 提出一种结合四种关键技术的算法：(1) 引入角度中间变量简化三角函数表达；(2) 将递归分解为子映射并做因子化处理；(3) 使用重缩放的高阶乘积/商规则；(4) 用截断形式幂级数的复合规则替代Faa di Bruno公式。算法在导数阶上具有O(r³)的算术复杂度。

Result: 基准测试表明：SymPy直接符号微分在导数阶r=14时达到20秒超时；Bell多项式递归在r>24时超过60秒超时；而所提方法在r=40时仍保持亚毫秒级计算时间，且在参考方法可行的区域内与数值结果达到机器精度一致。端到端验证为20拖车泊车操作的计算与动画演示。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效突破了传统符号微分和Faa di Bruno公式的计算瓶颈，将带任意数量拖车车辆的微分平坦性前馈转向计算从不可行变为实时可行，为复杂车辆系统的轨迹生成提供了实用化的数值工具。

Abstract: We address the practical computation of feedforward steering inputs for a car with n trailers via differential flatness. The standard route (iterated symbolic differentiation of the flat output) scales prohibitively with n: even with computer-algebra software the expression sizes grow super-exponentially and become intractable beyond n approx. 5. We propose an algorithm that combines four ingredients (angular intermediate variables, factorisation of the recursion in submaps, rescaled higher-order product/quotient rules, and a composition rule based on truncated formal power series instead of Faa di Bruno's formula). We benchmark the proposed method against (i) direct symbolic differentiation in SymPy and (ii) Faa di Bruno's formula evaluated via Bell polynomials. Both reference methods hit a clear computational wall: SymPy reaches a 20 s timeout at derivative order r = 14, while the Bell-polynomial recursion exceeds a 60 s timeout beyond r = 24. The proposed approach has O(r3) arithmetic complexity in the derivative order and remains under one millisecond up to r = 40, with numerical agreement to machine precision in the regime where the reference methods succeed. As an end-to-end illustration, a 20-trailer parking manoeuvre is computed and animated.

</details>

Result: 文章梳理了四个研究方向的核心理念与关键成果，指出多臂老虎机、自整定调节器和遗憾率最小化控制器已有长期且丰富的文献积累，而极小极大最优双控制器提供了一种有前景的鲁棒双控制框架。

Conclusion: 该论文是对线性时不变系统参数未知条件下双控制理论与方法论的综述，系统对比了四大研究方向，并强调极小极大最优双控制作为未来鲁棒双控制发展的新兴研究方向。

Abstract: The term "dual control" refers to the dual objective of simultaneously balancing exploration and exploitation. Problems of this kind have been studied for nearly a century. This paper is devoted to theory and methodology relevant for optimal control of linear time-invariant systems whose parameters are initially unknown and must be learned by active probing. We review the main ideas underlying four major research directions: Multi-armed bandits, self-tuning regulators, regret rate minimizing controllers, and minimax optimal dual controllers. The first three have a long history and rich literature, whereas the fourth provides a promising framework for robust dual control.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发展了一条从线性可控性到矩阵李群上非线性可控性的自洽李理论路径，通过李括号、单参数子群和BCH公式等工具，将局部代数信息传播为可达集的全局结论，并特别关注旋转群SO(n)上的可控几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：在矩阵李群上，局部代数信息（如无穷小方向）能否被传播为关于可达集的全局陈述。线性情形由Kalman秩条件等工具解决，但在非线性情形中，相关的李代数可能是无穷维的，有限维李对应可能失效，因此需要新的理论框架。

Method: 从线性可控性出发（矩阵指数、Cayley-Hamilton定理、轨迹公式、Kalman族B,AB,...,A^{n-1}B），推广到非线性情形，用李括号替代矩阵幂。以SO(n)为具体实验室，利用斜对称矩阵、指数映射、交换子、单参数子群和Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式展示无穷小方向的传播。发展右不变系统的可达子群论证，给出Yamabe步骤的详细证明序列，并分析上三角矩阵代数的可解、幂零和理想实例。最后与Sussmann的一般非线性系统局部可控性理论进行对比。

Result: 提供了SO(3)控制的数值模拟以展示受控旋转的几何结构，并给出SO(4)和SO(7)的显式交换子序列使局部控制方向的传播可视化。建立了从有限维机制到一般非线性局部可控性理论的完整对照框架。

Conclusion: 文章为从线性可控性到非线性矩阵李群可控性提供了一个系统、自洽且具教学意义的李理论路径，阐明了有限维线性代数工具如何被李代数工具替代，以及在何种条件下这种替代能够成立，同时通过与Sussmann理论的对比揭示了该框架的适用范围与局限性。

Abstract: This article develops a self-contained Lie-theoretic route from linear controllability to nonlinear controllability on matrix Lie groups. The organizing question is whether local algebraic information can be propagated into statements about reachable sets. The linear case supplies the model: the matrix exponential, Cayley-Hamilton theorem, trajectory formula and Kalman family B,AB,...,An-1B reduce controllability to finite-dimensional linear algebra. In the nonlinear setting, Lie brackets replace matrix powers, but the associated Lie algebra may be infinite dimensional and the finite-dimensional Lie correspondence can fail. Particular emphasis is placed on rotation groups. Skew-symmetric matrices, their exponentials, commutators, one-parameter subgroups, and the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula provide a concrete laboratory for seeing how infinitesimal directions propagate on SO(n). A numerical SO(3) simulation illustrates the geometry of controlled rotations, while explicit SO(4) and SO(7) commutator sequences make the propagation of a localized control direction visible. The article then develops the attainable-subgroup argument for right-invariant systems, gives a detailed proof sequence behind the Yamabe step, and works through solvable, nilpotent and ideal examples for upper-triangular matrix algebras. The finite-dimensional mechanism is finally contrasted with Sussmann's local controllability theory for general nonlinear systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 首先将问题形式化为一个基于测度的规划程序，并利用矩-sum-of-squares（moment-SOS）框架对其进行松弛求解；随后引入相应的对偶问题，将其表述为泛函规划，再进一步强化为sum-of-squares（SOS）程序，从而获得可计算的概率上界。

Result: 通过多个案例研究验证了所提出方法的有效性，其中包括一个轨道物体（卫星/空间物体）的典型案例，证明了该方法在实际应用中的可行性和准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法将对偶的SOS结构与经典屏障函数技术相类比，但其区别在于提供的是概率性安全证书而非确定性证书，这为在部分统计信息条件下进行系统安全验证提供了新的理论工具和计算框架。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of estimating upper bounds on the probability that a dynamical system will enter an undesirable region at some point within a finite time horizon. The primary source of uncertainty lies in the system's initial state, for which only a finite set of moments is known or within a prescribed interval. To tackle this problem, we formulate a measure-based program and propose its relaxation using the moment-sum-of-squares (moment-SOS) framework. The corresponding dual problem is introduced as a functional program, which is subsequently strengthened into a sum-of-squares (SOS) program. Notably, this dual formulation bears a structural resemblance to classical barrier function techniques for certifying system safety, with the key distinction that it yields a probabilistic certificate. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through multiple case studies, including a case involving an object in orbit.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间动态投资组合优化问题，在终端损失的CVaR约束下，利用CVaR的辅助阈值表示建立了最优策略的存在性和强对偶性，并提出一种基于对偶的双层嵌套（二分-黄金分割搜索）算法求解，证明了算法的收敛性，数值实验揭示了终端CVaR约束导致非对称跨状态再配置等关键行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实投资者常面临终端损失的风险约束（如CVaR），但连续时间动态投资组合优化在CVaR约束下的理论分析与高效算法仍不完善，市场不完备情形下尤其困难。作者旨在为一般凸交易目标下带CVaR约束的动态优化提供理论基础与可计算的求解算法。

Method: 利用CVaR的辅助-阈值表示建立最优策略存在性与强对偶性，不依赖市场完备性假设；据此设计基于对偶的嵌套算法，外层采用二分法搜索阈值、内层采用黄金分割搜索Lagrange乘子，内层迭代归结为标准无约束随机控制问题；并证明迭代策略随迭代次数趋于无穷收敛至最优控制。

Result: 数值实验表明：当风险约束非有效时恢复Merton最优策略；约束有效时最优策略呈状态依赖特征，投资者在不利结果后降低风险暴露，但在有利结果后保持甚至临近到期时增加风险暴露，即终端CVaR约束产生跨状态的非对称再配置而非均匀去风险；无交易禀赋风险放大保守调整，价格冲击降低期望持仓与调整速度。

Conclusion: 终端CVaR约束下的最优策略并非简单地全面去风险，而是呈现出与状态相关的非对称调整模式；所提出的对偶嵌套算法在一般凸交易目标与市场不完备下均具有理论保证与实际收敛性，为带路径无约束的动态风险约束优化提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We study continuous-time dynamic portfolio optimization under a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraint on the investor's terminal loss. For a general class of convex trading objectives, we exploit the auxiliary-threshold representation of CVaR to establish the existence of an optimal strategy and strong duality without requiring market completeness. These results motivate a dual-based nested bisection--golden-search algorithm over the threshold and Lagrangian multiplier, where the inner iterations reduce to standard unconstrained stochastic control problems. We prove that the resulting strategies converge to the optimal control as the number of iterations tends to infinity. Numerical experiments recover the Merton policy when the risk constraint is nonbinding. When the constraint is binding, the optimal strategy becomes state dependent: the investor reduces risky exposure following adverse outcomes but preserves, and near maturity may increase, exposure following favorable outcomes. Thus, a terminal CVaR constraint produces an asymmetric reallocation across states rather than uniform de-risking. Nontraded endowment risk amplifies the conservative adjustment, whereas price impact lowers desired positions and adjustment speeds.

</details>


### [63] [Direct vs. Indirect Data-Driven Control: Case-study of Switching Systems Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20207)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于间接方法的切换线性系统稳定性分析框架，从含噪声的状态测量数据中利用二次Lyapunov分析进行稳定性判定，并结合机器学习泛化界、系统辨识和灵敏度分析，提供了有限样本收敛率保证。同时扩展了直接方法以支持有界噪声以外的场景，并通过数值实验表明在中等至较高噪声水平下，间接方法比直接方法具有更紧的概率保证和更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动控制中直接方法已有大量有限样本保证结果，但间接方法（先辨识模型再进行基于模型的控制）在切换线性系统的数据驱动稳定性分析中缺乏类似的有限样本保证，这构成了方法论上的空白。

Method: 采用间接方法，结合机器学习中的泛化界、系统辨识技术以及二次Lyapunov分析的灵敏度分析，构建一个统一的框架，为切换线性系统提供基于含噪状态测量的稳定性分析，并推导有限样本收敛率保证。此外，扩展现有的直接数据驱动方法以覆盖有界噪声之外的测量噪声情形，以便公平比较。

Result: 数值实验表明，在中等至较高噪声水平下，间接方法相比直接方法提供更紧的概率保证，并且对噪声和离群值具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 间接数据驱动方法在含噪声场景下的切换线性系统稳定性分析中表现出优于直接方法的性能，具有更紧的有限样本保证和更强的鲁棒性，为数据驱动控制的方法论选择提供了重要参考。

Abstract: A central methodological question in data-driven control is whether to adopt a direct or indirect approach. Direct methods infer a controller or certificate directly from data, while indirect methods first identify a system model and then apply model-based control techniques. Recent developments of the direct method have led to finite-sample guarantees for the data-driven stability analysis of switched linear systems under various settings. However, for the indirect method, such guarantees remain largely elusive. In this paper, we provide a novel framework for the stability analysis of switched linear systems from noisy state measurements, using the indirect approach and quadratic Lyapunov analysis. Our framework comes with finite-sample guarantees on the convergence rate of the system. For that, we combine generalization bounds from machine learning and system identification with sensitivity analysis from quadratic Lyapunov analysis. To enable comparison, we also extend existing direct data-driven methods to handle measurement noise beyond the bounded noise case currently available in the literature. Finally, we compare the two approaches through numerical experiments, revealing that under moderate-to-high noise levels the indirect approach yields tighter probabilistic guarantees as well as greater robustness to noise and outliers than the direct approach

</details>

*Weihao Mao,Jane J. Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Asplund空间中建立了值函数方向次微分的变分分析框架，通过引入新的方向性条件解决无限维空间中有界集缺乏紧致性的问题，推导出值函数方向极限次微分和奇异次微分的上界估计，为无限维分层系统的灵敏度分析提供了精细化分析基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 值函数的方向次微分能定量衡量最优值对扰动的响应，但现有结果主要局限于有限维设置。无限维空间（如Asplund空间）中有界集缺乏紧致性，这使得方向非光滑分析向无限维扩展面临本质困难，需要新的理论工具来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 本文在Asplund空间中构建系统的变分分析框架，建立关键的方向微积分法则，将方向非光滑分析推广到无限维。针对无限维空间中紧致性缺失的问题，引入了一个新的方向性条件（directional condition），在此条件下推导值函数的方向极限次微分和方向奇异次微分的上界估计。

Result: 建立了Asplund空间中值函数方向次微分的完整变分框架，包括方向微积分法则；引入新方向性条件并在此条件下获得了值函数方向极限次微分与方向奇异次微分的上界估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为无限维分层系统的灵敏度分析提供了精细化且严格的数学基础，填补了方向非光滑分析在无限维空间中的理论空白，拓展了变分分析的应用范围。

Abstract: Directional subdifferentials of the value function provide a quantitative measure of optimal value response to perturbations. While existing results are largely limited to finite-dimensional settings, this paper develops a comprehensive variational framework in Asplund spaces. We establish essential directional calculus rules, extending directional nonsmooth analysis to infinite dimensions. To address the lack of compactness of bounded sets in infinite-dimensional spaces, we introduce a new directional condition, under which we derive upper estimates for directional limiting and singular subdifferentials of the value function. These results provide a refined analytical foundation for sensitivity analysis in infinite-dimensional hierarchical systems.

</details>

### [65] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督式机器学习模型的直升机起飞重量估算方法，利用空客全球机队的海量数据，并遵循EASA ML应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准建立了学习保证流程。研究提出了机器学习需求、模型描述，以及基于长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络的实现方案，并在验证需求后成功实现在传统航电计算机上部署，适用于机载告警等关键功能。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用监督式机器学习模型，基于空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集进行训练。研究遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准，构建了一套学习保证流程，包括：提出机器学习需求（ML Requirements）、编写机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description），并选用长短期记忆（LSTM）循环神经网络实现重量估算模型。最终对实现进行了需求验证。

Result: 该LSTM模型实现成功通过了对机器学习需求的验证，并且能够在传统航电计算机上进行部署，证明了所提出的机器学习重量估算器适用于机载关键功能（如机载告警）的实际应用场景。

Conclusion: 本文展示了在航空安全关键领域应用机器学习模型的可行性，通过遵循EASA和Eurocae的适航框架，实现了可部署的直升机起飞重量估算器，为机器学习在航空机载系统中的工程化落地提供了实践参考。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [66] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三角模糊数重标度距离（d_TR），该度量将线性重标度（LRE）直接集成到距离计算中，解决了异构属性（不同尺度或单位）下模糊数比较需预先归一化的问题。作者证明了d_TR满足度量的所有性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，适用于合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法和多准则决策辅助等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统决策常涉及三角模糊数（TFNs）表示的不精确信息，而量化TFN间距离是关键步骤。现有大多数距离度量假设所有值处于同一尺度，在应用于不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需要额外的归一化预处理阶段，这在实践中增加了复杂性和潜在误差。

Method: 提出Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_TR)度量，其核心创新是将线性重标度（LRE）直接嵌入距离计算过程中，在模糊数比较的同时自动完成归一化。文章通过形式化数学证明验证了d_TR满足度量的四项基本性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并额外证明了其有界性、尺度不变性与原点不变性。此外，该度量支持加权向量以对各维度进行优先级排序。

Result: 形式化证明了d_TR是一个有效的度量（满足所有度量公理），并具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性等理想性质。这些性质使其在异构模糊数据处理中无需单独预处理阶段即可直接使用。

Conclusion: d_TR为异构模糊数据场景提供了一种无需预归一化的距离度量方案，结合维度加权能力，在合成指标构建、距离驱动的机器学习算法及多准则决策辅助等领域具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [67] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在动态心电图（ECG）上的时间推理和诊断报告生成能力不足问题，提出了大规模多模态动态ECG数据集Holtercare-23K（含22,980个问答对，来源于788份临床Holter记录，具备信号-视频-文本三模态对齐）和基于此的评测基准Holtercare-Bench，用于评估模型在时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个任务上的表现。零样本评估显示主流MLLMs在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距，而微调可带来大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在医学应用中表现优异，但大多只处理静态图像或短期信号，在动态ECG这一关键领域缺乏复杂时间推理和诊断报告生成能力，主要受限于高质量数据集和基准的缺失。

Method: （i）构建Holtercare-23K数据集：从788份临床Holter记录中生成22,980个医学问答对，提出信号-视频-文本三模态对齐机制；（ii）提出Holtercare-Bench基准：从时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度评估模型的动态ECG理解能力。

Result: 对主流MLLMs的零样本评估揭示其在处理超长病理序列方面存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后取得了大幅性能提升，验证了数据集和基准的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前MLLMs在电生理学任务上的局限性，为长期医学MLLMs提供了基础性基准，并公开了项目资源。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [68] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了量子-经典混合机器学习在基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的应用。通过多种角度编码和纠缠策略构建量子核，结合SVM和逻辑回归模型，发现量子核模型在片段组学数据集上可超越经典SVM基线，支持量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT肺癌筛查虽能降低死亡率，但受限于依从性和管理挑战。基于cfDNA的生物标志物提供补充路径，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测困难，需要更强大的机器学习方法来捕捉这些复杂模式。

Method: 使用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化两个数据集，特征选择后使用20和40特征子集。采用角度和密集角度特征映射将特征编码到量子希尔伯特空间，配合多种纠缠策略。基于精确态矢量模拟计算保真度量子核，并集成到预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归中，与原始特征SVM基线对比。

Result: 量子核模型在两个数据集上均取得竞争性表现。片段组学上多个20特征配置的AUC优于经典SVM基线；甲基化上经典SVM获得最高AUC，但部分量子模型保持竞争力且某些情况下提高了特异性。将特征从20增至40并未持续改善性能，反而常增加变异。

Conclusion: 量子核方法作为cfDNA肺癌检测的互补方案具有潜力，编码和纠缠设计对分类性能有系统性影响，未来值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [69] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用数据集来增强大语言模型（LLM）不确定性量化（UQ）的方法：通过简单的分类器，使用已有的不确定性得分和相似查询的正确性作为特征，预测LLM响应是否正确，从而在计算开销极小的情况下持续优于现有的零样本UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型不确定性量化方法（如口头化置信度和多代生成）大多是零样本的，虽然不需要标注数据即可产出不确定性得分，但在实际部署前仍需在目标数据集上评估其性能。作者希望利用这个评估过程所获得的数据来进一步提升UQ表现。

Method: 构建简单的分类器来预测LLM响应的正确性，使用两类特征：1）现有的不确定性得分；2）相似查询的正确性信息（即与当前查询相似的查询是否正确的特征）。该方法基于部署前的数据集进行训练，计算开销极小。

Result: 实验表明，利用目标数据集后，该方法在一致性上优于现有的零样本不确定性得分方法，且仅带来极小的额外计算开销。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种低成本、易实现的不确定性量化增强方案，通过利用部署前的数据集信息，能够为LLM在实际应用中的不确定性量化提供持续的性能提升。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [70] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）框架，统一了patching、梯度、Hessian-vector积和子集干预等机制可解释性测量方法，将其形式化为y=Ax+w的线性测量结构，并给出实用的迭代测量流程，在多个模型和任务上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性追求模型不直接暴露的量（表示状态、组件效应、交互和干预响应），但现有测量方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-vector积、子集干预）在不同访问假设下测量不同目标量，缺乏统一的结构化描述与系统性的测量策略。

Method: 将各类测量统一为机制断层扫描：在选定基和干预族下，测量形式为y=Ax+w，其中A为干预矩阵、x为目标映射、w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基误设。提出实用流程：从代价最低的测量开始，在预期尺度上的留出干预上测试，校准简单失配，当存在结构化残差时扩展测量族。在两HMM、Tracr、GPT-2-small IOI和Qwen-2.5-7B上验证。

Result: 控制误差随观察者误差上升，目标改善可能掩盖干扰状态运动；前向访问下稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching用更少干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探针改善局部归因映射；提升测量和Hessian-vector积恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互；GPT-2-small IOI上Name Mover-Negative Name Mover交互是三个跨组对中最大的留出预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B上有限校准使加性拒答响应映射足够，留出误差不支持配对提升。

Conclusion: 机制断层扫描提供了一个统一的测量框架，能够指导研究者根据访问条件和目标选择合适的测量族，并通过迭代校准和扩展来高效恢复内部机制，但所需测量族依赖于具体模型和基的选择。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [71] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于模板的神经网络鲁棒性验证加速技术的有效性及其局限性，提出了一种新的"联合稳定神经元"度量来解释模板在不同场景下子sumption率差异大的现象，并提出了FastCert技术，该技术自动跨层分配模板并在模板不可能带来加速时完全摒弃模板。在基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务上，FastCert相较现有模板复用技术实现了平均1.13倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在多个关键领域的应用使得其鲁棒性验证越来越重要。不完整验证技术可以通过跨查询复用中间层抽象状态（即模板）来实现加速，但模板加速方法在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的鲁棒性仍不明确，需要系统性研究其有效性边界。

Method: 首先对不同场景下模板子sumption率进行大规模系统性研究；提出"联合稳定神经元"（jointly stable neurons）这一新度量来解释模板加速效果在不同场景下的巨大差异；在此基础上提出FastCert技术，自动将模板分布到神经网络各层以最大化性能影响，并在预测模板不会带来加速时完全放弃使用模板。

Result: 在基于覆盖设计的大规模L0验证任务集合上，FastCert相较现有基于模板的复用技术实现了平均1.13倍的加速。

Conclusion: 模板技术在不同场景下的有效性高度依赖网络架构和训练方式等条件；基于联合稳定神经元度量的FastCert能够智能决策模板的使用与分配，在提高加速效果的同时避免了模板无效场景下的性能浪费。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [72] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出疾病连续体定位（DCP）框架，一种基于纵向贝叶斯学习的模型，利用纵向扩散张量成像（DTI）连续估计阿尔茨海默病（AD）的疾病严重程度，并衍生出疾病连续体评分（DCS）以量化个体在AD连续体中的位置及不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD是一个连续的生物学进展过程，但现有基于神经影像的AI方法大多局限于从横断面影像进行离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的连续演变特性，缺乏能够连续反映疾病进展的量化表征。

Method: DCP将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，通过联合整合纵向观测数据与弱临床监督进行推断，并引入纵向贝叶斯学习框架，由此推导出疾病连续体评分（DCS）用于量化个体在AD连续体上的位置及其不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明，DCP在代表性疾病进展方法中表现一致最优；综合验证分析显示DCS能够准确刻画疾病严重程度，具有良好的临床相关性，能保持纵向疾病演化特征，并可预测未来的疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种基于影像的定量表征，能够超越传统的诊断标签和临床评分，实现对阿尔茨海默病进展的连续评估，为AD的临床研究和实践提供新的工具。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [73] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EventTime是一个多分辨率框架，结合长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据，通过事件融合模块和动态对比学习目标，来预测网络安全漏洞披露等网络传播冲击事件后短期异常财务损失。作者还构建了SECURE真实数据集，实验证明该方法优于现有时间序列和事件感知基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全漏洞披露等事件会通过网络传播造成金融市场剧烈异常损失，但现有时间序列预测模型主要关注趋势、季节性和自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件。同时，高影响事件稀疏和背景市场噪声进一步加剧了挑战。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率框架，融合长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据；包含事件融合模块将时间表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关的近期市场模式；引入动态对比学习目标，在训练中构造事件感知和时间序列感知的正负样本对以缓解稀疏监督问题。

Result: 在SECURE数据集和实验中，EventTime在估计事件后财务损失方面持续优于最先进的时间序列和事件感知基线；进一步分析显示其表示更具事件敏感性、对不完整元数据更鲁棒，且对网络安全披露后的短期市场影响估计更具可解释性。

Conclusion: EventTime为事件条件冲击预测提供了一个有效框架，通过结合多分辨率时间序列信息和事件元数据，加上动态对比学习策略，能够在稀疏事件监督下实现准确、鲁棒且可解释的事件后异常损失估计。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [74] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，将表格数据正常状态知识转化为统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型，通过提示词驱动代码生成来构建异常检测评分引擎，在24个表格数据集上超越15个SOTA基线，且无需微调或神经网络训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本往往表现为跨特征依赖关系的违反，而非简单的边际偏差。现有检测器依赖几何或重建信号，而先前基于LLM的方法主要需要用正常样本微调LLM或生成合成异常，这些方法缺乏对特征间结构化关系的建模能力，且计算成本高。

Method: LLM-Detector利用LLM的上下文学习能力进行结构化、提示条件约束的评分合成。具体流程：(1)将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型；(2)将这些知识组织成提示词用于代码生成；(3)生成的评分引擎评估统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。该设计完全无需LLM微调或神经网络训练。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上评估，与15个SOTA基线对比，在混合类型和纯连续数据设置下均取得一致的性能提升。同时由于无需微调和神经网络训练，计算成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector通过将LLM的上下文学习能力与结构化正常状态知识相结合，实现了高效、低成本的表格数据异常检测，在保持高检测性能的同时消除了传统方法中昂贵的训练环节，为实际工业场景中的表格异常检测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [75] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模对照实验（3,933次运行）系统比较了三种模型刷新策略（定期重训、错误阈值触发、ADWIN统计漂移触发）与不重训基线在概念漂移下的表现，发现最关键的设计决策不是重训策略本身，而是模型是否支持增量学习。在增量学习场景下，任何策略与基线无显著差异；在非增量场景下，周期性重训显著优于响应式策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但从业者缺乏何时重训的原则性指导。重训成本高昂、预算有限，且训练与部署延迟会导致模型在数据持续变化时仍提供陈旧预测，因此需要系统性研究不同重训策略的效用。

Method: 构建统一系统模型，将重训预算和训练加部署延迟显式化。在3,933次实验运行中，跨3种漂移情景、3种预算水平、最多5种延迟水平、3个数据集和2种学习模式，对照评估周期性重训、错误阈值触发重训、ADWIN统计漂移触发重训三种策略与不重训基线。

Result: 增量学习场景下，54组成对比较中无任何策略与基线存在实际显著差异；非增量场景下，策略选择导致漂移后准确率相差15-55个百分点。周期重训在突变型与渐变型漂移下显著优于两种响应式策略，而响应式策略仅在重复型漂移下保有优势。还发现响应式策略的系统性失败模式及延迟-预算排队交互效应使有效重训预算静默减半。

Conclusion: 模型刷新策略的选择应优先考虑是否采用增量学习机制；在非增量场景中，简单周期重训是更稳健的默认选择。研究发布完整模拟器、数据管道和每次运行的产物以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [76] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeltaMomentum，利用线性层梯度的键值结构，通过规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向以其出现频率决定遗忘速率，作为优化器动量缓冲的即插即用替代品，在预训练、图像分类等多个任务上显著加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（如AdamW、SGD）的动量均采用指数移动平均（EMA）更新，对所有方向以固定速率遗忘。然而深度学习训练中输入的分布高度各向异性，少数方向被频繁查询而大多数方向很少出现。已有方法通过在缓冲外增加额外处理来应对这一各向异性，但动量更新规则本身并未改变。作者希望将方向感知直接构建进动量更新规则中。

Method: 观察到线性层的梯度可分解为"输入作为键、输出侧误差作为值"的键值结构。利用这一结构，DeltaMomentum采用规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向按其出现频率设定遗忘速率。理论上证明其是合法动量，无需矩阵求逆即可进行输入侧曲率修正，且在固定和漂移的最优点下比EMA更快清除陈旧方向。作为动量缓冲的即插即用替代品，其系数在μP下可跨宽度迁移，额外计算量仅为gated-MLP块线性成本的22.2%-25.0%，且无需持久内存。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，AdamW+DeltaMomentum（DeltaAdamW）在67M规模下比AdamW节省高达46.39±4.32%的训练步数达到相同验证损失，370M规模下节省22.12±0.80%，1B规模在Chinchilla最优预算下增益保持。Muon基线在同一协议下不如DeltaAdamW。在SGD、ResNet-18和ViT-Tiny的CIFAR-10任务上同样有效。训练时诊断验证了预测的机制（更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向）。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过将方向感知内建到动量更新规则中，有效解决了训练中梯度方向各向异性问题，在多种优化器、模型架构和任务规模下均优于传统EMA动量方法，且额外计算开销可控、无需持久内存，是实用且有效的动量更新替代方案。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种操作能力层级和"Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC)"协议，用于检验不同模态编译器（如音频和加速度）在未见过表面上的表征是否具有可互换的物理语义。在Cluster Haptic数据集上，正确配对的响应距离比错误配对近4.5倍；在受控弹塑性系统中，预注册预算下执行器无法推进程序，但在收敛预算下实现了执行（oracle NMSE 0.18），14/16项检查通过。结果表明属性访问、响应替换、融合闭包和有序执行是可分离、可独立测试的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探针无法确定不同传感器是否携带相同的可执行语义，也无法检验这种语义在新动作组合下是否存活。需要建立操作性能力层级的判别性测试。

Method: 提出Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC)协议：在独立训练的模态编译器上，测试其能否在冻结的响应图表之外（即未见过的样本上）可互换地进入该图表。在Cluster Haptic上验证跨模态语义一致性，在受控弹塑性系统上验证有序执行能力，使用预注册预算和收敛预算分别测试。

Result: Cluster Haptic上，正确表面配对的音频与加速度表征在响应空间中比错误配对近4.5倍（19个未见过表面全部保持）。预注册预算下执行器无法推进程序；收敛预算下rank-three图表成功执行程序（oracle NMSE 0.18），14/16注册检查通过，两个失败源于融合信息矩阵的某种对角限制表现与完整矩阵相当。

Conclusion: 消除门槛是执行器的属性而非图表的属性——发出整个程序而非共享逐步动态的执行器比实体盲预测器差38倍。非可辨识性结果解释了为什么压缩和融合本身无法确定未见的组合律。属性访问、响应替换、融合闭包和有序执行是四个可分离且可分别测试的能力层级。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart. A matching non-identifiability result explains why compression and fusion alone cannot determine an unseen composition law. These results separate attribute access, response substitution, fusion closure, and ordered execution into distinct, separately testable achievements.

</details>


### [78] [Empirical Characterization of Learning Geometry in Hybrid Quantum Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19497)
*Sandra Leticia Juárez-Osorio,Jorge I. Hernandez-Martinez,Jesus Ivan Ruiz-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*
